## Table Directory & Summary

| # | Table Name | Source File | Records | Primary / Foreign Keys | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `brand_rules` | `brand_rules.csv` | 16 | rule_id | Master compliance rules enforcing brand voice, terminology, legal boundaries, and claim constraints. |
| 2 | `rule_versions` | `rule_versions.csv` | 12 | id (Foreign Key: rule_id -> brand_rules.rule_id) | Version history and audit log of rule changes, previous rule texts, patterns, and authors. |
| 3 | `facts` | `facts.csv` | 13 | fact_id | Ground-truth verified product specifications and factual statements used for claims verification. |
| 4 | `kb_sources` | `kb_sources.csv` | 4 | source_id | Source documents ingested into the knowledge base (e.g., brand guidelines, pricing sheets). |
| 5 | `kb_chunks` | `kb_chunks.csv` | 13 | chunk_id (Foreign Key: source_id -> kb_sources.source_id) | Segmented text chunks from KB sources with 768-dim vector embeddings for hybrid RAG search. |
| 6 | `kb_meta` | `kb_meta.csv` | 1 | key | Knowledge base global metadata and version tracking counter. |
| 7 | `scans` | `scans.csv` | 119 | scan_id | Primary scan records capturing readiness scores (0-100), overall status, and full evaluation payloads. |
| 8 | `scan_documents` | `scan_documents.csv` | 557 | scan_id | Detailed document scans with channel categorization, redacted text, file hashes, and evaluation results. |
| 9 | `scan_versions` | `scan_versions.csv` | 640 | version_id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id) | Historical revision snapshots of scanned text and readiness score progression during edits. |
| 10 | `scan_cache` | `scan_cache.csv` | 2 | cache_key | Content hash cache of scan analysis responses for fast, deterministic cache hits. |
| 11 | `claim_evidence` | `claim_evidence.csv` | 33 | id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id, chunk_id -> kb_chunks.chunk_id) | RAG claim verification evidence linking extracted claims to KB chunks with similarity scores and verdicts. |
| 12 | `flag_events` | `flag_events.csv` | 10 | event_id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id, rule_id -> brand_rules.rule_id) | Audit log of reviewer decisions (Accepted/Rejected/Dismissed) on flagged violations with justification reasons. |
| 13 | `scan_comments` | `scan_comments.csv` | 4 | comment_id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id) | Reviewer commentary and collaboration feedback threads on scan documents. |
| 14 | `approvals` | `approvals.csv` | 9 | approval_id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id) | Cryptographically signed audit approvals with SHA-256 text hashes, hash chain link, and digital signature. |

## SQL DDL Schema Definitions

```sql
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.brand_rules (
    rule_id INT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default', version_no INT DEFAULT 1,
    violation_type VARCHAR(50), severity VARCHAR(20), rule_cited TEXT, source_ref TEXT, rule_text TEXT,
    regex_pattern TEXT, default_fix TEXT, claim_status VARCHAR(50), allowed_numbers TEXT,
    applies_to VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'text'
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.facts (
    fact_id INT PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default', fact_text TEXT, source_ref TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.scans (
    scan_id VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY, readiness_score INT, status VARCHAR(30), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    input_type VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'text', payload JSONB
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.flag_events (
    event_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, scan_id VARCHAR(50), rule_id INT, action VARCHAR(20), reason TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_sources (
    source_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default', doc_title TEXT, source_type VARCHAR(20),
    content_hash VARCHAR(64), version_no INT DEFAULT 1, updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_chunks (
    chunk_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, source_id INT, workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default', doc_title TEXT,
    page_num INT DEFAULT 1, paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1, chunk_text TEXT, content_hash VARCHAR(64),
    embedding vector(768), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.claim_evidence (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, scan_id VARCHAR(50), claim_text TEXT, chunk_id INT, verdict VARCHAR(20),
    similarity FLOAT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.scan_documents (
    scan_id VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY, workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default', channel VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'general',
    redacted_text TEXT, file_sha256 VARCHAR(64), pdf_bytes BYTEA, result_json JSONB,
    reaudit_needed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE, reaudit_reason TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.scan_versions (
    version_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, scan_id VARCHAR(50), version_no INT, text_snapshot TEXT, score INT,
    actor_name VARCHAR(80), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), UNIQUE(scan_id, version_no)
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.scan_comments (
    comment_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, scan_id VARCHAR(50), author_name VARCHAR(80), author_role VARCHAR(20),
    comment_text TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.approvals (
    approval_id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, scan_id VARCHAR(50), version_no INT, text_sha256 VARCHAR(64), score INT,
    reviewer VARCHAR(80), role VARCHAR(20), prev_hash VARCHAR(64), entry_hash VARCHAR(64), signature TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.rule_versions (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY, rule_id INT, version_no INT, old_rule_text TEXT, old_pattern TEXT,
    changed_by VARCHAR(80), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.scan_cache (
    cache_key VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY, response_json JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.kb_meta (
    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
);

ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';
ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version_no INT DEFAULT 1;
ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS allowed_numbers TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.brand_rules ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS applies_to VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'text';
ALTER TABLE public.facts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';
ALTER TABLE public.scans ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS input_type VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'text';
ALTER TABLE public.scans ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payload JSONB;
ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reaudit_reason TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS workspace_id VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT 'default';
ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS channel VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'general';
ALTER TABLE public.scan_documents ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reaudit_needed BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS paragraph_num INT DEFAULT 1;
ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS doc_title TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS content_hash VARCHAR(64);
ALTER TABLE public.kb_chunks ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS embedding vector(768);
```

assertions).
[ERROR-T019] Unauthorized superlative: Copy uses 'The absolute best and undisputed #1 platform worldwide',
violating Brand Guideline Tone & Style Manual section 4.2.

#1 (Exact capitalization required: 'ClaimLoom').
[ERROR-M019] Deprecated terminology: Found reference to 'legacy client portal', violating Brand Rule #8 (Must use
'Creator Workspace' or 'Team Portal').

against the background (Minimum required: 4.5:1 for body text).
[ERROR-A019] Typography restriction: Disclaimer font size is 7.2pt, violating accessibility guidelines (Minimum required
size: 8.0pt).

### Table: `scan_versions`
- **Source File**: `datbase tables/scan_versions.csv`
- **Record Count**: 640
- **Primary Key**: version_id (Foreign Key: scan_id -> scans.scan_id)
- **Columns**: `version_id`, `scan_id`, `version_no`, `text_snapshot`, `score`, `actor_name`, `created_at`
- **Description**: Historical revision snapshots of scanned text and readiness score progression during edits.

Architecture
This document represents page 1 of the official ClaimLoom governance audit report. ClaimLoom ensures
rigorous compliance across all multi-channel marketing assets, combining real-time Two-Tier rule engines with
hybrid pgvector/BM25 retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) against the central Truth Vault. All claims are
cryptographically anchored and audited for tone, terminology, factual accuracy, accessibility, and visual
presentation.
Key Compliance Pillars & Operational Guidelines
• Pillar 1: Factual Grounding & Truth Vault Alignment — Every numeric claim, pricing structure, and product
feature statement must be directly verified against approved markdown documents in the enterprise repository.
Drift or unverified assertions trigger automatic high-severity flags and block publishing sign-off workflows.
• Pillar 2: Tone & Brand Consistency — Maintaining professional brand equity across public channels including
LinkedIn B2B posts, Instagram captions, email campaigns, and high-impact billboards. Prohibits absolute
guarantees and unverified superlatives.
• Pillar 3: Accessibility & Visual Redaction — Automated contrast checking, font size verification (minimum 8pt
standards), and PII masking prior to vector ingestion and LLM auditing.

1. Factual & Truth Vault Contradictions (High Severity)
[ERROR-F01] Pricing violation detected: Document states 'Pro Plan costs $15/month billed monthly', which
contradicts Truth Vault spec v1 (Mandatory Pricing Policy: $29/month or $290/year).
[ERROR-F011] Unverified core capability: Document asserts '100% crash-proof autonomous data pipeline with zero
latency', violating Truth Vault Fact #4 (SLA capped at 99.9% uptime with nominal latency variance).
2. Tone & Absolute Guarantee Violations (High Severity)
[ERROR-T01] Unsubstantiated absolute guarantee: Copy reads 'Guaranteed 100% risk-free compliance or your
money back instantly!', violating Brand Governance Rule #3 (Prohibition of absolute guarantees and risk-free
assertions).
[ERROR-T011] Unauthorized superlative: Copy uses 'The absolute best and undisputed #1 platform worldwide',
violating Brand Guideline Tone & Style Manual section 4.2.
3. Terminology & Branding Violations (Medium Severity)
[ERROR-M01] Incorrect capitalization/naming: Found 'claimloom' or 'Claim loom' in paragraph block 1, violating Brand Rule
#1 (Exact capitalization required: 'ClaimLoom').
[ERROR-M011] Deprecated terminology: Found reference to 'legacy client portal', violating Brand Rule #8 (Must use
'Creator Workspace' or 'Team Portal').
4. Accessibility & Contrast Deficiencies (Medium/Low Severity)
[ERROR-A01] WCAG 2.1 Contrast failure: Footer legal disclaimer on page 1 renders text with a contrast ratio of 2.4:1
against the background (Minimum required: 4.5:1 for body text).
[ERROR-A011] Typography restriction: Disclaimer font size is 7.2pt, violating accessibility guidelines (Minimum required
size: 8.0pt).
